\chapter{Business Enabling Capabilities and owned behaviour}\label{model:bec}
All 76 established BEC contexts have unresolved CT tiers and complete ancestry. This chapter groups them by documented contextual view and responsibility; its heading depth is navigation, not a CT assertion. Features, Functions, Services and Processes below retain their actual owning context. Function/Service/Process and Feature associations form a graph, recorded in \hyperref[model:relationships]{the relationship register}. A Feature listing is not a claim of complete CT1 → CT2 → CT3 → FT reconciliation.
